Agent，接成可管理、可复盘、可持续改进的数字员工。\\u003c/strong\\u003e\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003c/div\\u003e\\n\\u003chr\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eA lightweight state kernel and agent-agnostic local control plane for loop\\nengineering, LoopX keeps long-running work reviewable, restartable, and easier\\nto hand off across turns, tools, and agents. It does not replace your agent\\nruntime.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003eLoop engineering for long-running AI agents and peer agent teams.\\u003c/strong\\u003e\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cblockquote\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eKeep the loop moving. Long-running work is harder:\\nobjectives change, owner decisions appear, evidence goes stale, agents hand work\\nto peers, and a scheduler can keep spending after no useful transition remains.\\nChat memory and a timer are not enough to govern that.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eLoopX keeps the durable control state in one compact layer:\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cdiv class=\\\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\\\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\\\"objective / issue / project\\n   │\\n   ▼\\nLoopX state: objective + gates + todos + scope + evidence + quota\\n   │\\n   ├─ human judgment needed? ── yes ─▶ ask a concrete question and wait\\n   │\\n   ├─ safe fallback available? ──────▶ run one bounded agent slice\\n   │\\n   ▼\\nCodex / Claude Code / Cursor / shell agent executes one turn\\n   │\\n   ▼\\nwrite evidence + handoff + next todo ─▶ quota decides the next tick\\\"\\u003e\\u003cpre lang=\\\"text\\\" class=\\\"notranslate\\\"\\u003e\\u003ccode\\u003eobjective / issue / project\\n   │\\n   ▼\\nLoopX state: objective + gates + todos + scope + evidence + quota\\n   │\\n   ├─ human judgment needed? ── yes ─▶ ask a concrete question and wait\\n   │\\n   ├─ safe fallback available? ──────▶ run one bounded agent slice\\n   │\\n   ▼\\nCodex / Claude Code / Cursor / shell agent executes one turn\\n   │\\n   ▼\\nwrite evidence + handoff + next todo ─▶ quota decides the next tick\\n\\u003c/code\\u003e\\u003c/pre\\u003e\\u003c/div\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003ca target=\\\"_blank\\\" rel=\\\"noopener noreferrer\\\" href=\\\"/huangruiteng/loopx/blob/main/docs/assets/control-plane-board.svg\\\"\\u003e\\u003cimg src=\\\"/huangruiteng/loopx/raw/main/docs/assets/control-plane-board.svg\\\" alt=\\\"LoopX control-plane board\\\" style=\\\"max-width: 100%;\\\"\\u003e\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eA useful mental model is an\\n\\u003cstrong\\u003e\\u003ca href=\\\"/huangruiteng/loopx/blob/main/docs/development/control-plane-course/00-concept-primer.md\\\"\\u003eagent-native Kanban for long-running work\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/strong\\u003e.\\nCards carry identity, authority, evidence, and continuation. Moves are validated\\noperators such as claim, gate, monitor, and writeback. The board is a\\nprojection; LoopX state remains the source of truth.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eRegistered agents are peers. Claims, leases, task boundaries, capabilities, and\\ntyped continuation decide who acts next; no durable leader identity is\\nrequired.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eLoopX is useful when you run:\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cul dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003emulti-day engineering, research, benchmark, or experiment objectives;\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eissue and PR loops that must preserve scope, evidence, and review state;\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003erecurring heartbeat or monitor work;\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003eprojects with owner, safety, publication, or private-data gates;\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003epeer-agent teams where ownership, leases, and handoff matter;\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003cli\\u003ecreator, research, or operations workflows whose progress must remain\\nlegible to a non-engineering operator.\\u003c/li\\u003e\\n\\u003c/ul\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eLoopX is not an autonomous production controller. Dangerous permissions,\\npublishing, production writes, and final ownership stay with the human.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003ca id=\\\"user-content-see-it-in-action\\\"\\u003e\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cdiv class=\\\"markdown-heading\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003ch2 tabindex=\\\"-1\\\" class=\\\"heading-element\\\" dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eEvidence\\u003c/h2\\u003e\\u003ca id=\\\"user-content-evidence\\\" class=\\\"anchor\\\" aria-label=\\\"Permalink: Evidence\\\" href=\\\"#evidence\\\"\\u003e\\u003csvg data-component=\\\"Octicon\\\" class=\\\"octicon octicon-link\\\" viewBox=\\\"0 0 16 16\\\" version=\\\"1.1\\\" width=\\\"16\\\" height=\\\"16\\\" aria-hidden=\\\"true\\\"\\u003e\\u003cpath d=\\\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\\\"\\u003e\\u003c/path\\u003e\\u003c/svg\\u003e\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/div\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eThese are not one-turn demos. The public OpenViking contribution sequence and\\nthe redacted, owner-run Auto ML showcase each span\\n\\u003cstrong\\u003e200+ hours of elapsed loop lifetime\\u003c/strong\\u003e across many bounded turns, decisions,\\nand evidence updates. It is not a full agent platform, an agent runtime, or an autonomous\\nproduction controller.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eToday LoopX ships a durable state kernel for goals, typed todos and decision\\nscopes, peer claims and leases, evidence and writeback, quota-aware scheduling,\\nand cross-turn continuation. Guided start, recurring heartbeat, isolated Codex\\nCLI turns, evidence-backed Issue-Fix admission, optional Explore and auto\\nresearch paths, public validation canaries, and a read-first multi-project\\ndashboard build on that shared control state.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eSupport levels remain explicit. The state and CLI contracts are the stable\\ncenter; several host integrations and advanced paths are optional, default-off,\\nor experimental. LoopX does not grant credentials, approve destructive or\\nproduction actions, publish on a user's behalf without authorization, or turn\\nan unverified run into evidence of success.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eThe next milestones are simpler installation and host packaging, broader typed\\nruntime adapters, stronger terminal acceptance across repeated public loops,\\nindependent adoption and outcome evidence, and a more polished management\\nsurface.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cdiv class=\\\"markdown-heading\\\" class=\"react-directory-commit-message\"><a data-pjax=\"true\" title=\"fix(knn-smoke): replace wall-clock speedup oracle with deterministic … (#2847)\n\n* docs(knn-smoke): document why wall-clock speedup is non-deterministic across CI\n\n`time.perf_counter()` timing varies between hosts, making strict\n`score &gt; baseline_score` assertions unreliable as a test oracle.\nAdd a note explaining the design limitation — the evidence\nnormalization pipeline independently verifies improved/contradicted\nstatus and protected-scope checks, so the timing threshold is not the\nsole coverage gate.\n\n* fix(knn-smoke): replace wall-clock speedup oracle with deterministic contract\n\nThe old assertions checked `score &gt; baseline_score` which relies on\n`time.perf_counter()` — non-deterministic across CI hosts.  Replace\nthem with a semantic verification that:\n- `ok` is True (eval succeeded)\n- `score` is numeric and positive (valid contract shape)\n- the evidence pipeline independently judges improved/contradicted\n\nThe loop over (dev, holdout) ensures both splits get the same\nstructural validation without duplicating assertions.\n\n* fix(knn-workspace): force LF newlines for deterministic file hashes across platforms\n\n`path.write_text(encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;)` on Windows translates \\n to\n\\r\\n, which breaks SHA-256 hashes in `protected_scope_sha256`.\nThe contract hashes are computed from Python str constants (\\n), but\nthe files written to disk contain \\r\\n, making `protected_scope_clean`\nalways False on Windows.\n\nPass `newline=&quot;\\n&quot;` explicitly so the benchmark workspace files are\nbyte-identical across platforms.\n\n* fix(knn-smoke): decouple evidence normalization from wall-clock eval output\n\nThe previous fix moved the timing oracle downstream — the\n`metric.value &gt; baseline_metric` assertions at lines 164-167 still\nread from the same wall-clock eval payload, so the non-determinism\npersisted.\n\nThis commit restructures the smoke into three independent phases:\n\n1. **Evaluator correctness** — real eval must produce ok=True with a\n   numeric positive score, but timing-based score thresholds are NOT\n   asserted.\n\n2. **Evidence normalization (deterministic)** — new fixed public eval\n   fixtures (examples/fixtures/knn-eval-{dev,holdout}.public.json) with\n   known score &gt; baseline_score feed the evidence pipeline.\n   improved/contradicted status and protected-scope checks are verified\n   against these fixtures, making them reproducible across CI hosts.\n\n3. **Protected-scope violation** — mutating task.py and re-running\n   evidence against the same fixtures must produce contradicted status.\n   This check is independent of evaluator timing output.\n\nPortability: subprocess calls use encoding='utf-8' with errors='replace'\nand `sys.executable eval.py` instead of `bash eval.sh` to avoid\nWindows console-codepage and python3-store-stub issues.\" class=\"Link--secondary\" class=\"react-directory-commit-message\"><a data-pjax=\"true\" title=\"fix(global-registry): serialize global registry read-modify-write (#2825)\n\n* fix(global-registry): serialize global registry could interleave read -&gt; merge -&gt; write and let the\nlater writer commit a payload built before the earlier writer's goals\nexisted. The dropped goals belonged to the other process, so every sync\nstill reported `ok: true`, `wrote: true`, and its own id in\n`synced_goal_ids` with no route collision.\n\nThe existing `exclusive_file_lock` in `register-agent` guards\n`source_registry_path`, the per-project registry, so two projects take two\ndifferent locks and both mutate the one shared file.\n\nHold the global registry lock across the authoritative read, merge, and\nwrite. The pre-lock merge is kept only so the write-denied preflight can\nstill report a merge preview, and the writability probe stays ahead of the\nlock so an unwritable runtime root keeps returning the graceful\n`global_registry_write_denied` payload instead of raising on lock creation.\n`retire_global_registry_goals` had the same read-modify-write shape and now\nre-reads under the same lock rather than writing back its inspection\nsnapshot. (#2825)\n\n* fix(global-registry): serialize global registry read-modify-write\n\n`sync_project_registry_to_global` read `registry.global.json` and wrote it\nback with nothing serializing the two, so two `loopx` processes sharing one\n`common_runtime_root` could interleave read -&gt; merge -&gt; write and let the\nlater writer commit a payload built before the earlier writer's goals\nexisted. The dropped goals belonged to the other process, so every sync\nstill reported `ok: true`, `wrote: true`, and its own id in\n`synced_goal_ids` with no route collision.\n\nThe existing `exclusive_file_lock` in `register-agent` guards\n`source_registry_path`, the per-project registry, so two projects take two\ndifferent locks and both mutate the one shared file.\n\nHold the global registry lock across the authoritative read, merge, and\nwrite. The pre-lock merge is kept only so the write-denied preflight can\nstill report a merge preview, and the writability probe stays ahead of the\nlock so an unwritable runtime root keeps returning the graceful\n`global_registry_write_denied` payload instead of raising on lock creation.\n`retire_global_registry_goals` had the same read-modify-write shape and now\nre-reads under the same lock rather than writing back its inspection\nsnapshot. A `dry_run` preview takes no lock.\n\nRefs #2823\n\n* fix(global-registry): unify serialized mutations\n\n---------\n\nCo-authored-by: huangrt01 &lt;huangrt01@163.com&gt;\" class=\"Link--secondary\" href=\"https://huangruiteng.github.io/loopx/\" rel=\"nofollow\">Public website</a> · <a href=\"https://huangruiteng.github.io/loopx/docs/\" rel=\"nofollow\">Docs</a> · <a href=\"#try-loopx\">Try LoopX</a> · <a href=\"#evidence\">See real loops</a> · <a href=\"#why-loopx\">How it works</a> · <a href=\"https://my.feishu.cn/wiki/CaL5wMk9ui17ngkWzeUcMlAYnZg\" rel=\"nofollow\">User manual</a> · <a href=\"/huangruiteng/loopx/blob/main/README.zh-CN.md\">简体中文</a></p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>把会干活的 Agent，接成可管理、可复盘、可持续改进的数字员工。</strong></p>\n</div>\n<hr>\n<p dir=\"auto\">A lightweight state kernel and agent-agnostic local control plane for loop\nengineering, LoopX keeps long-running work reviewable, restartable, and easier\nto hand off across turns, tools, and agents. It does not replace your agent\nruntime.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Loop engineering for long-running AI agents and peer agent teams.</strong></p>\n<blockquote>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Keep the loop moving. Keep the judgment human.</p>\n</blockquote>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><a id=\"user-content-how-it-works\"></a></p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Why LoopX</h2><a id=\"user-content-why-loopx\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Why LoopX\" href=\"#why-loopx\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">An agent can finish a task in one session. Long-running work is harder:\nobjectives change, owner decisions appear, evidence goes stale, agents hand work\nto peers, and a scheduler can keep spending after no useful transition remains.\nChat memory and a timer are not enough to govern that.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">LoopX keeps the durable control state in one compact layer:</p>\n<div class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\"><pre lang=\"text\" class=\"notranslate\"><code>objective / issue / project\n   │\n   ▼\nLoopX state: objective + gates + todos + scope + evidence + quota\n   │\n   ├─ human judgment needed? ── yes ─▶ ask a concrete question and wait\n   │\n   ├─ safe fallback available? ──────▶ run one bounded agent slice\n   │\n   ▼\nCodex / Claude Code / Cursor / shell agent executes one turn\n   │\n   ▼\nwrite evidence + handoff + next todo ─▶ quota decides the next tick\n</code></pre><div class=\"zeroclipboard-container\">\n    <clipboard-copy aria-label=\"Copy code to aria-hidden=\"true\" data-component=\"Octicon\" height=\"16\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" data-view-component=\"true\" class=\"octicon octicon-check js-clipboard-check-icon 1.06 0Z\"></path>\n</svg>\n    </clipboard-copy>\n  </div></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><a target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\" href=\"/huangruiteng/loopx/blob/main/docs/assets/control-plane-board.svg\"><img src=\"/huangruiteng/loopx/raw/main/docs/assets/control-plane-board.svg\" alt=\"LoopX control-plane board\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"></a></p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">A useful mental model is an\n<strong><a href=\"/huangruiteng/loopx/blob/main/docs/development/control-plane-course/00-concept-primer.md\">agent-native Kanban for long-running work</a></strong>.\nCards carry identity, authority, evidence, and continuation. Moves are validated\noperators such as claim, gate, monitor, and writeback. The board is a\nprojection; LoopX state remains the source of truth.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Registered agents are peers. Claims, leases, task boundaries, capabilities, and\ntyped continuation decide who acts next; no durable leader identity is\nrequired.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">LoopX is useful when you run:</p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>multi-day engineering, research, benchmark, or experiment objectives;</li>\n<li>issue and PR loops that must preserve scope, evidence, and review state;</li>\n<li>recurring heartbeat or monitor work;</li>\n<li>projects with owner, safety, publication, or private-data gates;</li>\n<li>peer-agent teams where ownership, leases, and handoff matter;</li>\n<li>creator, research, or operations workflows whose progress must remain\nlegible to a non-engineering operator.</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\">LoopX is not an autonomous production controller. Dangerous permissions,\npublishing, production writes, and final ownership stay with the human.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><a id=\"user-content-see-it-in-action\"></a></p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Evidence</h2><a id=\"user-content-evidence\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Evidence\" href=\"#evidence\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">These are not one-turn demos. The public OpenViking contribution sequence and\nthe redacted, owner-run Auto ML showcase each span\n<strong>200+ hours of elapsed loop lifetime</strong> across many bounded turns, decisions,\nand evidence updates. Elapsed lifetime is wall-clock project time. It is\nnot 200 hours of continuous model execution or a claim of unattended\nproduction autonomy. Open each visual to\ninspect the public-safe graph, evidence branches, and decisions preserved\nacross turns.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Open-Source Issue Fix</h3><a id=\"user-content-open-source-issue-fix\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Open-Source Issue Fix\" href=\"#open-source-issue-fix\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>200+ hour public contribution arc: PR delivery and reusable fix knowledge\nevolve together.</strong></p>\n<a href=\"/huangruiteng/loopx/blob/main/docs/assets/long-running-loop-openviking-trajectory.png\">\n  <img src=\"/huangruiteng/loopx/raw/main/docs/assets/long-running-loop-openviking-trajectory.png\" alt=\"Open-source issue-fix trajectory linking focused PR delivery with reusable LoopX capabilities\" width=\"420\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\">\n</a>\n<p dir=\"auto\">LoopX's creator uses this path as an\n<a href=\"https://github.com/volcengine/OpenViking/pulls?q=is%3Apr+author%3Ahuangruiteng\">OpenViking contributor</a>.\nThe represented public contribution sequence spans more than 200 elapsed hours\nfrom its first PR creation to the latest represented review or update. The\n<a href=\"/huangruiteng/loopx/blob/main/docs/capabilities/issue-fix/README.md\">Issue-Fix capability</a> keeps rolling\nrepository context, revision-stamped fix knowledge, and reviewer-facing\npreferences separate; linked PRs plus current checkout source and tests remain\nauthoritative.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Auto ML Experiment</h3><a id=\"user-content-auto-ml-experiment\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Auto ML Experiment\" href=\"#auto-ml-experiment\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Redacted owner-run showcase: a 200+ hour experiment arc keeps hypotheses,\nmatched evidence, invalid lineages, running replicates, and promote/stop gates\nvisible in one graph.</strong></p>\n<a href=\"/huangruiteng/loopx/blob/main/docs/assets/long-running-loop-ml-experiment-trajectory.png\">\n  <img src=\"/huangruiteng/loopx/raw/main/docs/assets/long-running-loop-ml-experiment-trajectory.png\" alt=\"Auto ML Experiment trajectory with experiment lineages, evidence gates, and promotion decisions\" width=\"760\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\">\n</a>\n<p dir=\"auto\">The redacted public-safe graph preserves decision lineage across that 200+ hour\nelapsed window. It is an owner-run showcase, not a claim of continuous compute,\nindependent reproduction, a production result, or company or employer\nendorsement. The redacted image is not sufficient to reproduce the underlying\nexperiment independently.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Auto Research</h3><a id=\"user-content-auto-research\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Auto Research\" href=\"#auto-research\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Reproducible public KNN demo: proposer, executor, and evaluator/promoter agents\niterate in parallel while todo, quota, evidence, and targeted wake remain\nvisible.</strong></p>\n<a href=\"/huangruiteng/loopx/blob/main/docs/assets/auto-research-multi-agent-showcase.png\">\n  <img src=\"/huangruiteng/loopx/raw/main/docs/assets/auto-research-multi-agent-showcase.png\" alt=\"Auto Research multi-agent workspace with proposer, executor, evaluator/promoter, todo, quota, evidence, and targeted wake activity\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\">\n</a>\n<p dir=\"auto\">This screenshot comes from LoopX's built-in exact-KNN demo. class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Try LoopX</h2><a id=\"user-content-try-loopx\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Try LoopX\" href=\"#try-loopx\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Requirements: Python 3.11+, <code>curl</code>, <code>tar</code>, and a macOS or Linux shell. Git is\nonly needed for contributor clone/canary workflows. The Python package has no\nruntime dependencies outside the standard library.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Install without cloning:</p>\n<div class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">LoopX folds its control-plane mechanics into five questions:</p>\n<markdown-accessiblity-table data-catalyst=\"\"><table>\n<thead>\n<tr>\n<th>Question</th>\n<th>What LoopX keeps visible</th>\n</tr>\n</thead>\n<tbody>\n<tr>\n<td>What is the objective?</td>\n<td>The active goal, explicit scope, and current authority.</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>What happens next?</td>\n<td>Ordered user and agent todos, ownership, claims, and leases.</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>What needs human judgment?</td>\n<td>Concrete user gates instead of a vague \"waiting for owner.\"</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>What evidence changed?</td>\n<td>Compact run history, validation, blockers, and accepted writeback.</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>May the loop continue?</td>\n<td>Quota, capabilities, safe fallback, scheduler hints, and stop conditions.</td>\n</tr>\n</tbody>\n</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Control-Plane Surface</h3><a id=\"user-content-control-plane-surface\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Control-Plane Surface\" href=\"#control-plane-surface\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<markdown-accessiblity-table data-catalyst=\"\"><table>\n<thead>\n<tr>\n<th>Surface</th>\n<th>What it does</th>\n<th>Start with</th>\n</tr>\n</thead>\n<tbody>\n<tr>\n<td>Goal state and status</td>\n<td>Tracks active state, todos, claims, gates, evidence, run history, and first-screen attention.</td>\n<td><code>loopx status</code>, <code>loopx diagnose</code>, <code>loopx review-packet</code></td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>Quota and interaction contract</td>\n<td>Decides whether a turn should deliver, ask, wait, self-repair, or stay quiet.</td>\n<td><code>loopx quota should-run</code>, <a href=\"/huangruiteng/loopx/blob/main/docs/quota-allocation.md\">quota allocation</a></td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>Agent runtime bridges</td>\n<td>Keeps Codex App, Codex CLI, Claude Code, and generic workers aligned with the same guard.</td>\n<td><code>loopx heartbeat-prompt</code>, <code>loopx codex-cli-bootstrap-message</code>, <code>loopx worker-bridge</code></td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>Operator surfaces</td>\n<td>Renders compact status without making the browser the state authority.</td>\n<td><code>loopx serve-status</code>, <a href=\"/huangruiteng/loopx/blob/main/apps/presentation/dashboard/README.md\">dashboard</a></td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>External projections</td>\n<td>Projects todos and gates into collaboration surfaces while LoopX remains authoritative.</td>\n<td><code>loopx lark-kanban</code>, <a href=\"/huangruiteng/loopx/blob/main/docs/integrations/lark-kanban-control-plane-adapter.md\">Lark Kanban adapter</a></td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>Domain capabilities</td>\n<td>Packages repeatable work lanes such as issue fixing, content operations, value connector planning, ML experiment advice, benchmark evidence, and Explore.</td>\n<td><code>loopx issue-fix</code>, <code>loopx content-ops</code>, <code>loopx value-connectors</code>, <code>loopx ml-experiment</code>, <code>loopx benchmark</code>, <a href=\"/huangruiteng/loopx/blob/main/docs/capabilities/explore/README.md\">Explore</a></td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>Experimental context learning</td>\n<td>Lets named registered agents trial provider-neutral Reward Memory through ignored, default-off project configuration. OpenViking is one provider option, not a global dependency.</td>\n<td><code>loopx reward-memory experiment-status</code>, <a href=\"/huangruiteng/loopx/blob/main/docs/reference/protocols/reward-memory-architecture-v0.md\">Reward Memory architecture</a></td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>Governance patterns</td>\n<td>Captures reusable routing, gate, evidence, projection, and planning shapes.</td>\n<td><a href=\"/huangruiteng/loopx/blob/main/docs/concepts/interaction-pattern-catalog.md\">interaction patterns</a>, <a href=\"/huangruiteng/loopx/blob/main/docs/state-interaction-model.md\">state model</a></td>\n</tr>\n</tbody>\n</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>\n<p dir=\"auto\">The shipped primitives include lifetime goals, concrete user gates, audited safe\nfallbacks, peer todo ownership, quota and steering, compact run history,\nevidence-backed handoff, a read-first management surface, project-level value\nsignals, and public/private boundary checks.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Runtime Responsibilities</h3><a id=\"user-content-runtime-responsibilities\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Runtime Responsibilities\" href=\"#runtime-responsibilities\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<markdown-accessiblity-table data-catalyst=\"\"><table>\n<thead>\n<tr>\n<th>Role</th>\n<th>Responsibility</th>\n</tr>\n</thead>\n<tbody>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>Agent</strong></td>\n<td>Plans, analyzes, uses tools, and performs one bounded action through a host/runtime.</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>Provider</strong></td>\n<td>Calls external systems and returns observations, effect results, and readback.</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>Capability</strong></td>\n<td>Defines the caller outcome, normalizes provider output, validates it, and proposes a typed transition.</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>Kernel</strong></td>\n<td>Owns durable todos, gates, monitors, accepted writeback, quota, recovery, and scheduling.</td>\n</tr>\n</tbody>\n</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>\n<p dir=\"auto\">The execution path is <code>Agent -&gt; Capability -&gt; Provider</code>; the control path\nreturns <code>Provider readback -&gt; Capability transition -&gt; Kernel</code>. An extension is\nhow an optional provider is packaged and managed, not another control-plane\nowner. See <a href=\"/huangruiteng/loopx/blob/main/docs/architecture.md\">Architecture</a> and\n<a href=\"/huangruiteng/loopx/blob/main/docs/reference/extensions.md\">Extensions and Capabilities</a>.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Advanced Paths</h2><a id=\"user-content-advanced-paths\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Advanced Paths\" href=\"#advanced-paths\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">The first useful loop does not require every optional surface. Quiet skips, preflight failures, and dry-run previews do not spend.\nWhen a user gate blocks one lane, a separately audited safe fallback may\ncontinue, but it must not bypass the gate.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Peer agents use <code>loopx todo claim</code> before delivery and <code>loopx todo update</code>\nafter validation so ownership and evidence remain visible.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Scheduler cadence follows <code>quota should-run.scheduler_hint</code>; installed Codex\nApp automations acknowledge the current hint through the returned\n<code>ack_hint.cli_args</code>. Collision recovery, monitor semantics, self-repair, and\nthe exact operator commands are maintained in\n<a href=\"/huangruiteng/loopx/blob/main/docs/guides/getting-started.md\">Getting Started</a>,\n<a href=\"/huangruiteng/loopx/blob/main/docs/quota-allocation.md\">Quota Allocation</a>, and\n<a href=\"/huangruiteng/loopx/blob/main/docs/operations/long-task-cadence-policy.md\">Long-Task Cadence Policy</a>.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Before publishing public docs or examples:</p>\n<div href=\"/huangruiteng/loopx/blob/main/AUTHORS.md\">Authors and Contributors</a>, and\n<a href=\"/huangruiteng/loopx/blob/main/docs/project/history.md\">Project History</a>.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">LoopX keeps local active state separate from the public repository. Do not\ncommit <code>.loopx/</code>, <code>.codex/goals/</code>, live <code>ACTIVE_GOAL_STATE.md</code>, raw benchmark\ntraces, credentials, private logs, or operator artifacts.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Current Status</h2><a id=\"user-content-current-status\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Current Status\" href=\"#current-status\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">The v0.4.x line is an early but usable local control plane for long-running\nagent work. It is not a full agent platform, an agent runtime, or an autonomous\nproduction controller.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Today LoopX ships a durable state kernel for goals, typed todos and decision\nscopes, peer claims and leases, evidence and writeback, quota-aware scheduling,\nand cross-turn continuation. Guided start, recurring heartbeat, isolated Codex\nCLI turns, evidence-backed Issue-Fix admission, optional Explore and auto\nresearch paths, public validation canaries, and a read-first multi-project\ndashboard build on that shared control state.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Support levels remain explicit. The state and CLI contracts are the stable\ncenter; several host integrations and advanced paths are optional, default-off,\nor experimental. LoopX does not grant credentials, approve destructive or\nproduction actions, publish on a user's behalf without authorization, or turn\nan unverified run into evidence of success.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">The next milestones are simpler installation and host packaging, broader typed\nruntime adapters, stronger terminal acceptance across repeated public loops,\nindependent adoption and outcome evidence, and a more polished management\nsurface.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Star History</h2><a
